# Squared tori from periodic tilings (genus 1)

Genus-1 analogue of Brooks-Smith-Stone-Tutte: a periodic Euclidean tiling, quotiented
by its own translation lattice, is a graph on a torus. There is no battery edge; the
choice is a class in H^1(T;R) = R^2, and each integral class (m,n) gives a square
tiling of a flat torus. Every result below is certified by the area identity
sum(side^2) = covolume of the image lattice, plus an exact no-overlap test.

corpus: 5422 records from 14 files · quotient edge cap 60 · 77 coprime classes each
